STANDARDS

Data Papers

Published datasets and the files their repositories serve

88 percent

of the Earth System Science Data papers indexed here keep their released files on Zenodo, 600 of 685. In Data in Brief it is 43 percent, 238 of 553.

Division: HERMES

Papers published since January 2024 in Scientific Data, Earth System Science Data, Data in Brief and GigaScience, each listed with the files its repository serves: Zenodo, Figshare, Mendeley Data, PANGAEA and OSF, read through each repository's public interface. Files were listed, never downloaded. A paper is listed when its repository record returns a file list. Papers whose data sits where no public file list can be read, such as GitHub or Dataverse, wait for a hand look. Studies funded by defence, military or intelligence agencies of any country are left out. Civilian science funders are kept. The newest papers in each journal come first, and older ones are being added. The classification is for indexing, not an assessment. Removal on request, any time.
